- It iterates many but maybe not enough. It normalizes sum 1, objective 2nM. Could incorporate exact constraints to target? Maybe use squared smoothing: minimize M + λ avg? Or minimize log-sum-exp of coefficients to approach max.
- It uses multiple inits but final limited. Could run longer on parent with more peaks and lower temperature.
- It doesn't exploit LP approach from SOTA. We can implement active-set subgradient from all peaks, maybe with line search.

We can design an optimization loop:
- Work with a normalized sum 1.
- Objective score=2n max(c). Lower is better.
- Use mirror descent: a_{new}= softmax( log a + η * something? To reduce c peaks, we need decrease coordinates that contribute to high c. Gradient of f=max c is positive for contrib coords. Projected gradient on simplex via mirror: a_new ∝ a * exp(-η g). Where g = 2 correlation(a, w) (positive). But note derivative of c_l wrt a_j =2a_{l-j}, positive, so moving mass away from high-contributing positions should reduce peaks. Mirror descent with -ηg.
- Step size η maybe 1/√t, c0 tune. But logsumexp objective is convex? c max is nonconvex? But local descent. Need avoid zeros; keep floor.
- Could instead use multiplicative weights: a_j <- a_j * exp(-η g_j). This is like previous.
- Use Nesterov on log variables maybe.

However we need guarantee improvement. Maybe implement multiple optimization strategies:
1. Exact smooth-max mirror descent with annealed temperature, full FFT gradient (fast). Run many iterations.
2. Coordinate descent / active peak moves with exact evaluations.

- capacity_update(a0, time_limit, margin=1.0, eta0=1.0, verbose, rng):

Detailed smooth_descent:
```
def smooth_descent(a0, time_limit, temp_start=1.0, temp_end=0.15, step0=1.0, restart_iters=2000):
    a = norm_sum(np.asarray(a0,float))
    n = len(a); N=next_pow2(2*n-1); L=2*n-1
    t0=time.monotonic()
    x=a.copy(); x_prev=a.copy()
    best_a=x.copy()
    c=fast_conv(x); best_m=c.max(); best_score=2*n*best_m
    stall=0; restart=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    for it in range(1, 10000000):
        if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit*0.95: break
        beta=(it-1.0)/(it+1.5) if it>1 else 0
        y=x + beta*(x-x_prev)
        y=np.maximum(y,1e-12)
        sy=y.sum()
        if sy<1e-12: y=x.copy()
        else: y=y/sy
        # compute c and weights
        A=np.fft.rfft(y,N)
        c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
        c=np.maximum(c,0)
        cmax=float(c.max())
        cur_score=2*n*cmax
        if cur_score<best_score:
           # evaluate x? Since y may be x with Nesterov; if cur_score uses y, track y if lower. Also compute x_score maybe.
           # To be safe, if cur_score < best, compute score for x too; pick lower.
           c_x=fast_conv(x)
           if 2*n*c_x.max() < 2*n*cmax:
               best_a=x.copy(); best_score=2*n*c_x.max(); best_m=...
           else:
               best_a=y.copy(); best_score=cur_score
           stall=0
        else: stall+=1
        temp_factor = temp_start + (temp_end-temp_start)*min(1.0, it/ (restart_iters*3))
        T = temp_factor*cmax
        if T <= 1e-15: T=1e-12
        scaled=(c-cmax)/T
        # clip scaled below -50 to avoid underflow? exp okay but weighted sum.
        scaled=np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0)
        w=np.exp(scaled)
        wsum=w.sum()
        if wsum<=0 or not finite: w=np.ones(L)/L
        else: w=w/wsum
        W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
        corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W, N)[:n]
        grad=2*corr
        # finite
        grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
        if grad.max() <= grad.min()+1e-15:
             # no change; perturb
        # center
        gmin=grad.min(); gmax=grad.max(); grange=gmax-gmin
        if grange <=1e-12:
             step_factor=0.0
        else:
             # choose eta_base = step0 / (sqrt(it)+1) / (1+grange*10?)
             # Since log update exp(-eta*g), exp(-eta*range) change. We want max ratio maybe 0.2 to 10.
             eta = step0 / (np.sqrt(it)+1.0)
             # scale by range: if range small, bigger? Use eta_eff = eta * (1.0 / (1.0 + grange*50.0))? Hmm.
             # Suppose grange ~0.01, eta=1 -> exp(-0.01) small. We want exp(-~0.1) maybe. So eta_eff=eta*10/(1+grange*10)? If grange=0.01, factor ~10/1.1=9 -> range exponent 0.09. If grange=0.1, factor 10/2=5 ->0.5. If grange=1, factor 10/11~0.9 ->0.9. Good.
             eta_eff = eta * 10.0 / (1.0 + 50.0*grange) ? Let's tune.
        candidate = np.exp(log_y - eta_eff*(grad-gmin))
        candidate = np.maximum(candidate,1e-30); normalize.
        # Line search multipliers? Use [0.5,1.0,2.0]? Choose best score.
        best_trial_score=cur_score; best_trial=candidate
        for mult in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0]: maybe cheaper: compute candidate with mult and score. But FFT each.
           trial=...; M=fast_conv(trial).max(); if 2nM < best_trial_score: update.
        # Accept if best_trial_score < cur_score (descent). If not, maybe accept with small probability? To escape, use best and stall.
        if best_trial_score < cur_score:
             x_prev=x; x=best_trial
        else:
             # small perturbation in log space
             # keep x for Nesterov, but maybe x= candidate*0.5?
             x_prev=x
             # stagnation
        if stall>restart_iters:
             # reset x to best or best perturbed
             strength=0.02+0.01*min(restart,5)
             x=best_a*(1+strength*rng.normal(0,1,n)); x=np.maximum(x,1e-12); x/=x.sum(); x_prev=x.copy()
             stall=0; restart+=1
             # increase step? step0 = min(2, step0*1.1)
        # optional: every 1000, if x score > best, blend toward best
    return best, best_score
```

Let's define top-level `smooth_descent(a0, time_limit, step_rate=1.5, temp_start=0.6, temp_end=0.05, restart_every=1500, seed=123)`.

Pseudo:
```
def smooth_descent(a0, time_limit, step_rate=1.5, temp_start=0.6, temp_end=0.05, restart_every=1500, verbose=False, rng_seed=123):
    a=norm_sum(a0); n=...; N...; L...
    x=a.copy(); x_prev=x.copy()
    best=x.copy()
    c=fast_conv(x); best_m=c.max(); best_score=2*n*best_m
    stall=0; restart_count=0; rng=...
    last_print=0
    for it in range(1, 10000000):
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       if it==1: beta=0 else beta=(it-1)/(it+1.5)
       y=x + beta*(x-x_prev)
       y=np.maximum(y,1e-12); y=y/y.sum()
       A=np.fft.rfft(y,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
       cmax=float(c.max())
       cur_score=2*n*cmax
       if cur_score < best_score:
           # check x as well? maybe not; if y better, y is within candidate. But y may be Nesterov not a valid step? It is nonnegative normalized, valid. We can accept.
           best=y.copy(); best_score=cur_score; best_m=cmax; stall=0
       else: stall +=1
       temp_factor = temp_start + (temp_end-temp_start)*min(1.0,(it-1)/(restart_every*2.0))
       T = temp_factor*cmax
       if T <= 1e-15: T=1e-12
       scaled=(c-cmax)/T
       w=np.exp(np.clip(scaled,-50,0))
       ws=w.sum()
       if ws<=0 or not finite: w=np.ones(L)/L
       else: w=w/ws
       W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
       corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,N)[:n]
       grad=2*corr
       grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, 0.0, 1e6, 0.0)
       # Maybe subtract mean? center min
       gmin=float(grad.min()); gmax=float(grad.max()); range=gmax-gmin
       if range <= 1e-14:
           # no useful gradient; small random move
           x=torch? no.
           if stall > 10: x= best.copy()
           x_prev=x.copy()
           continue
       eta = step_rate / ((np.sqrt(it)+1.0)*range)
       # cap eta to avoid overflow: if eta*range = step_rate/(sqrt+1) <= step_rate, okay.
       log_y = np.log(np.maximum(y,1e-300))
       cand = np.exp(log_y - eta*(grad-gmin))
       cand = np.maximum(cand,1e-30)
       cs=cand.sum()
       if cs<1e-15: cand=y.copy()
       else: cand=cand/cs
       # maybe blend with x if Nesterov jump too large? cand = (0.5)*x+0.5*cand? Could hurt. Use direct.
       c_c=fast_conv(cand); cand_m=c_c.max(); cand_score=2*n*cand_m
       if cand_score < cur_score:
           x=cand; x_prev=y? In Nesterov, previous x should be old x, not y. Usually x_prev=x_old; x_new=cand. But gradient computed at y. Set x_prev=x (old), x=cand.
       else:
           # accept step if not too bad? Maybe Blend 0.2
           half=(0.8*x+0.2*cand); half=norm_sum
           m=fast_conv(half).max(); if 2n*m < cur_score: x=half else keep
           # if keep, x_prev=x
       if stall > restart_every:
           x=np.maximum(best,1e-12)
           if restore? perturb =rng.normal(0, 0.01+0.005*min(restart_count,5), n)*x
           x=np.maximum(x+perturb,1e-12); x=x/x.sum(); x_prev=x.copy()
           stall=0; restart_count +=1
           # maybe step_rate *=1.1? track.
       if verbose and ... print
    return best,best_score
```

Now, implement next_pow2 efficiently:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

fast_conv_rfft:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    # small negatives
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
But repeated computing N; we can pass N,L to internal loops to avoid. Define `fast_conv_padded(a,N,L)`? Or have `conv_score(a,N=None)`. For helper optimizers, set N,L once. For external score, use fast_conv.

Function `score_internal(a)` computes sum and max:
```
def score_sequence_internal(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if len==0 return inf
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return inf
   L=... N...
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=irfft(A*A,N)[:L]; m=c.max()
   return 2*len(a)*m/(s*s)
```
For normalized, s~1. It may be used many times; but optimizers have local N.

def safe_evaluate(seq):

Interpolate:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
   f=np.asarray(f,float); f=np.maximum(f,0); s=f.sum(); if s<1e-15: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
   f=f/s
   old_x=np.linspace(0,1,len(f)); new_x=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   out=np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
   out=np.maximum(out,0); ss=out.sum(); if ss<1e-15: out=np.ones(new_n)/new_n else out=out/ss
   return out
```

Spectral flat root function:
```
def spectral_flat_root(n, alpha=0.0):
   L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(L); target=np.ones(L); if alpha>0? target = target**... (1)
   C=np.fft.fft(target,N)
   A=np.sqrt(C+0j)
   a=np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
   return norm_sum(a)
```

Let's write spectral_gs robust:
```
def spectral_gs(a0, time_limit, alpha0=0.25, alpha1=0.85, blend0=0.35, freq_blend=0.35, eps_frac=1e-8, verbose=False, rng_seed=1):
    a=norm_sum(a0); n=...; N=...; L=...
    best=a.copy()
    c=fast_conv(a) # or use full once
    best_m=c.max(); best_score=2*n*best_m
    current=a.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic(); it=0; stall=0; bad=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    warmup=200
    anneal=1000
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it+=1
       A=np.fft.fft(current,N)
       S=A*A
       c=np.fft.ifft(S).real[:L]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       cur_m=float(c.max())
       cur_score=2*n*cur_m
       if cur_score < best_score:
           best=current.copy(); best_score=cur_score; best_m=cur_m; stall=0
       else: stall+=1
       # If c sum not 1 due to current norm? csum= c.sum(); if csum<1e-12 break
       progress=min(1.0, float(it)/float(max(1,anneal)))
       alpha = alpha0 + (alpha1-alpha0)*progress
       eps = max(1e-12, eps_frac/L * (c.mean() if c.mean()>0 else 1.0/L))
       target=np.power(c+eps, alpha)
       # add fill uniform
       fill = 0.05 * (1.0/L)
       target = target + fill
       ts=target.sum()
       if ts<=0: target=np.ones(L)/L
       else: target=target/ts
       Ct=np.fft.fft(target, N)
       R=np.ones_like(S, dtype=complex)
       mask=np.abs(S) > 1e-14
       if np.any(mask):
          ratio=Ct[mask]/S[mask]
          # limit ratio magnitude to avoid huge: abs<=1000
          absr=np.abs(ratio)
          # if abs >1000 scale down
          big=absr>1000
          if np.any(big): ratio[big]/=absr[big]*0.9? Need vector: ratio = np.where(absr<1000, ratio, ratio/(absr*1.01))
          R[mask]=1.0 + freq_blend*(ratio - 1.0)
       # compute A_new
       A_new=A*np.sqrt(R)
       a_ideal=np.fft.ifft(A_new).real[:n]
       # try blends
       blend=min(1.0, blend0*(0.5+0.5*progress))
       candidates_blend = (blend, 0.15, 0.05)
       best_trial=current; best_trial_score=cur_score
       for bl in candidates_blend:
           trial=(1-bl)*current + bl*a_ideal
           trial=np.maximum(trial,0.0)
           ss=trial.sum()
           if ss<1e-12: continue
           trial=trial/ss
           c_t=np.fft.fft(trial,N); c_vals=np.fft.ifft(c_t*c_t).real[:L]
           # instead of full FFT for trial, use rfft fast? Use fast_conv maybe N passed.
           m_t=float(c_vals.max())
           sc=2*n*m_t
           if sc < best_trial_score:
              best_trial=trial; best_trial_score=sc
       if best_trial_score < cur_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
           current=best_trial
           stall=0
           bad=0
       else:
           bad+=1
           if bad > 50:
              bad=0
              # reduce blend/freq? We don't have variables? blend0 local can't modify? It can.
              blend0 = max(0.05, blend0*0.7)
              freq_blend = min(0.9, freq_blend*1.2)
           if stall > 800:
              # reset to best with perturbation
              strength=0.01 + 0.005*(stall//1600)
              current=best.copy()
              pert=rng.normal(0, strength, n)*current
              current=np.maximum(current+pert,0)
              current=current/current.sum()
              stall=0
       # If verbose print every 200
       if verbose and it%500==0: print(...)
    return best, best_score
```

Let's implement smooth_descent with a parameter `line_search=False` default. If line_search true, evaluate 2 candidates. We may use only for final small time.

Pseudo for smooth_descent more concrete:
```
def smooth_descent(a0, time_limit, step_rate=2.5, temp_start=0.6, temp_end=0.05, restart_iters=2000, decay_iters=8000, verbose=False, seed=123):
   a=norm_sum(a0); n=...; N=...; L=...
   x=a.copy(); x_prev=x.copy()
   best=x.copy()
   c=fast_conv_N(x,N,L); best_m=c.max(); best_score=2*n*best_m; cur_score=best_score
   t0=...; stall=0; restart=0
   rng=...
   for it in range(1, 10000000):
       if time limit break
       if it ==1: beta=0.0
       else: beta=(it-1.0)/(it+1.5)
       y=x + beta*(x-x_prev)
       y=np.maximum(y,1e-15); s=y.sum()
       if s<1e-15: y=x.copy()
       else: y=y/s
       A=np.fft.rfft(y,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       cmax=c.max(); cur_score=2*n*cmax
       if cur_score < best_score:
           best=y.copy(); best_score=cur_score; stall=0
       # compute temp
       frac=min(1.0, it/(restart_iters*3.0))
       tf=temp_start+(temp_end-temp_start)*frac
       T=tf*cmax
       if T<=1e-15: T=1e-12
       scaled=(c-cmax)/T
       w=np.exp(np.clip(scaled,-50,0))
       ws=w.sum()
       if ws<=0: w=np.ones(L)/L
       else: w=w/ws
       W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
       corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,N)[:n]
       grad=2*np.maximum(corr,0.0)
       gmin=grad.min(); grange=grad.max()-gmin
       if grange <= 1e-14:
           # small random movement
           if stall > 20:
              x=np.maximum(best*(1+0.01*rng.normal(0,1,n)),1e-12); x=x/x.sum()
              x_prev=x.copy()
           continue
       eta = step_rate / ((1.0 + it/decay_iters)*grange)
       # cap eta so max exponent <= max_exp maybe 5
       max_exp = min(4.0, step_rate/(1.0+it/decay_iters))? If using formula, eta*grange= step_rate/(1+it/decay). cap to 4.
       if eta*grange > 4: eta*=4/(eta*grange)
       log_y=np.log(np.maximum(y,1e-300))
       cand=np.exp(log_y - eta*(grad-gmin))
       cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-300)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s<1e-15: cand=y.copy()
       else: cand=cand/s
       # Evaluate candidate
       B=np.fft.rfft(cand,N)
       cc=np.fft.irfft(B*B,N)[:L]
       cand_m=cc.max(); cand_score=2*n*cand_m
       # Optionally if cand_score > cur_score, try smaller half step
       if cand_score >= cur_score:
           cand2=np.exp(log_y - 0.4*eta*(grad-gmin)); ... eval
           if cand2_score < cur_score: cand=cand2
       # Accept if cand_score < cur_score (descent) or stochastic.
       accept = cand_score < cur_score
       if not accept and cand_score < best_score*1.0005 and rng.random() < 0.1: accept=True  # small exploration
       if accept:
           # Nesterov: x_prev = x; x = cand
           x_prev=x
           x=cand
       else:
           # keep x but reduce Nesterov? x_prev=x to damp acceleration
           x_prev=x
       stall internal: if cur_score not best? use best_score.
       if best_score improved reset stall else stall++ (above)
       if stall > restart_iters:
           x=best.copy()
           strength=0.02+0.01*min(restart,5)
           x=np.maximum(x*(1+strength*rng.normal(0,1,n)),1e-12); x=x/x.sum()
           x_prev=x.copy()
           stall=0; restart+=1
           # maybe increase temp_end? no
   return best,best_score
```
But if we only accept descent, candidate with Nesterov may often not descend because y not on trajectory. We could accept candidate if cand_score < cur_score + tol to explore. But too much.

Maybe we should set `x_prev = x` before update? In FISTA, y = x + beta(x-x_prev), then gradient map at y -> z, x_new = z, x_prev = x. We do that. Good.

Need ensure `grad` has units of objective contribution per coordinate. If weights sum1, grad sum maybe ~1. Step formula with range could make max exponent step_rate/(1+it/decay). Good.

Could incorporate sum constraint exactly? Mirror exponent and normalization. Adding constant to gradient cancels. We subtract min, so all exponents 0..max_exp. This may push high-grad coords lower by up to e^{-max_exp}. Good.

Now, temporal budget. We'll make propose_candidate sequential with time checks. Need top-level helper functions with verbose maybe False.

Pseudo propose:
```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    HARD = min(float(budget_s)-30.0, 1740.0)
    best = None; best_score=inf
    parent=None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=float)
       p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
       if p.ndim==1 and p.size>0 and np.isfinite(p).all() and p.sum()>1e-12:
          parent=p; best=norm_sum(p); best_score=score_sequence_internal(best)
          # maybe also safe_eval? internal okay
    except: pass
    if best is None:

Let's define helper `conv_rfft_sequence(a,N,L)` top-level:
```
def conv_rfft_sequence(a,N,L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```

Implement generic:
```
def hermitize_spectrum(X, N):
    X = X.copy()
    X[0] = X[0].real
    if N % 2 == 0:
        X[N//2] = X[N//2].real
        if N//2 > 1:
            left = X[1:N//2]
            right = X[-1:-(N//2):-1]
            X[1:N//2] = 0.5*(left + np.conj(right))
            X[-1:-(N//2):-1] = np.conj(X[1:N//2])
    else:
        half = (N+1)//2
        left = X[1:half]
        right = X[-1:-(half-1? )? ]
```

def norm_sum(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   if a.ndim !=1: a=a.ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0) # but replacing nan maybe; for input we already finite. max(0).
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s < 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
       n=a.size if a.size>0 else 1
       return np.ones(max(1,int(n)), dtype=np.float64)/max(1, ...)
   return a/s
```

Let's detail functions.

`norm_sum(a)`:
```
def norm_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s
```
Does replacing inf with0 safe? For input parent finite.

`fast_conv(a)`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1: a=a.ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],float)
    L=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    # Remove tiny negative
    c[c<0]=0.0? vectorized c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
For performance, in loops use local `_fast_conv_N(a,N,L)`:
```
def fast_conv_N(a,N,L):
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
   return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
Define top-level.

`score_internal(a)`:
```
def score_internal(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   s=a.sum()
   if not finite or s<1e-15: return inf
   L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(L)
   c=fast_conv_N(a,N,L)
   m=float(c.max())
   if not finite(m): return inf
   return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```

`safe_evaluate(seq)`:
```
def safe_evaluate(seq):
    try:
       out=[float(x) for x in seq]
       return float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
       return float('inf')
```
If seq np array large conversion. okay.

`interpolate_sequence(f,new_n)`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f,new_n):
   f=np.asarray(f,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   new_n=int(new_n)
   if new_n <=0: new_n=1
   if f.size == new_n: return norm_sum(f)
   f=norm_sum(f)
   if f.size ==1:
      return np.full(new_n, f[0]/new_n)?? Wait normalized sum over old; to maintain sum1, if old one weight, distribute equally new? If f=[1], new uniform [1/new_n].
   old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,f.size)
   new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
   out=np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
   out=np.maximum(out,0)
   ss=out.sum()
   if ss<1e-15: out=np.ones(new_n)/new_n
   else: out=out/ss
   return out
```

`initial_shape(n, kind)`:
```
def initial_shape(n, kind=0):
  n=int(max(1,n))
  if kind==0: return norm_sum(np.ones(n))
  elif kind==1: x=np.linspace(0,1,n); return norm_sum(1+0.5*x)
  elif kind==2: x=np.linspace(0,1,n); return norm_sum(1+1.0*x)
  elif kind==3: i=np.arange(1,n+1,float); return norm_sum(i**0.35)
  elif kind==4: i=np.arange(1,n+1,float); return norm_sum(i**0.7)
  elif kind==5: x=np.linspace(0,1,n); return norm_sum(np.exp(-((x-0.4)/0.25)**2))
  elif kind==6: # spectral flat root
     L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(L); target=np.ones(L); C=np.fft.fft(target,N); A=np.sqrt(C+0j); a=np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]; return norm_sum(a)
  else: return norm_sum(np.ones(n))
```
Maybe for kind6, root of uniform target maximum at high freq, a may have negative large. Clipping maybe yields something.

`make_sparse_initial(n, m, kind, seed)`:
```
def make_sparse_initial(n, m, kind=0, seed=1):
   n=int(n); m=int(m); m=max(1,min(m,n))
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   a=np.zeros(n)
   if kind==0:
      idx=rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
   elif kind==1:
      idx=np.zeros(m,dtype=int); seen=set(); t=0; q=0
      # quadratic residues modulo n: need unique; choose a,b random 1..n-1
      while q<m and t<10*m:
         r=(a*t*t + b*t) % n
         if r not in seen:
            seen.add(r); idx[q]=r; q+=1
         t+=1
      if q<m:
         # fill random
         remaining=set(np.arange(n))-seen
         rem=list(remaining)
         take=np.random.default_rng(seed+7).choice(rem, size=m-q, replace=False)
         idx[:m]=? careful. Simpler if fail, random.
      m=q
   elif kind==2:
      idx=np.sort(rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False))
   else: random
   # weights: kind 3 geometric? Let's add param? Maybe based on kind:
   if kind <=2:
      w=np.ones(m)
      # slight random
      w = w * np.exp(0.2*rng.normal(size=m))
   else:
      w = np.exp(rng.normal(size=m)*0.5)
   a[idx]=w
   return norm_sum(a)
```

`hermitize_spectrum(X)`:
```
def hermitize_spectrum(X):
    X=np.array(X, dtype=np.complex128, copy=True)
    N=len(X)
    if N==0: return X
    X[0]=X[0].real
    if N % 2 == 0:
       X[N//2]=X[N//2].real
       if N//2 >1:
          left=X[1:N//2]
          right=X[-1:-(N//2):-1]
          avg=0.5*(left+np.conj(right))
          X[1:N//2]=avg
          X[-1:-(N//2):-1]=np.conj(avg)
    else:
       half=(N+1)//2
       if half>1:
          left=X[1:half]
          right=X[-1:-(N//2+1):-1]
          avg=0.5*(left+np.conj(right))
          X[1:half]=avg
          X[-1:-(N//2+1):-1]=np.conj(avg)
    return X
```
Check odd slice for N=3: half=2, left index1. right slice [-1:-(3//2+1): -1] = -1:-2:-1? stop -2 index1 exclusive -> index2 length1? For N=3 pairs 1 and2; stop -2 (index1) exclusive gives 2? yes. Good. N=5: half=3, left 1,2. right slice -1:-3? N//2+1=3? stop -3 index2 exclusive -> 4,3 length2. Good.

` spectral_gs` detailed code. Need local N,L. For trial c, use `fast_conv_N`. 
Pseudo code exact:
```
def spectral_gs(a0, time_limit, alpha0=0.30, alpha1=0.85, freq_blend=0.35, blend0=0.35, verbose=False, seed=17):
    a=norm_sum(a0)
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(L)
    best=a.copy()
    c=fast_conv_N(a,N,L)
    best_m=float(c.max())
    best_score=2*n*best_m
    current=a.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic()
    it=0; stall=0
    anneal=800
    bad=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
        it +=1
        A=np.fft.fft(current, N)
        S=A*A
        c=np.fft.ifft(S).real[:L]
        c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
        cur_m=float(c.max())
        cur_score=2*n*cur_m
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best=current.copy(); best_score=cur_score; best_m=cur_m; stall=0
        else:
            stall +=1
        if it == 0 or cur_m <= 1e-300: break
        progress = min(1.0, float(it)/float(max(1, anneal)))
        alpha = alpha0 + (alpha1-alpha0)*progress
        mean_c = float(c.mean()) if c.size>0 else 1.0/L
        if mean_c <=0: mean_c=1.0/L
        eps = max(1e-12, 1e-8*mean_c)
        target = np.power(c + eps, alpha)
        target = target + (0.02 / float(L))  # fill
        ts = float(target.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ts) or ts <= 0:
            target = np.ones(L)/float(L)
        else:
            target = target / ts
        Ct = np.fft.fft(target, N)
        R = np.ones(N, dtype=np.complex128)
        mask = np.abs(S) > 1e-12
        if np.any(mask):
            ratio = Ct[mask] / S[mask]
            absr = np.abs(ratio)
            # cap magnitude at 50
            too = absr > 50.0
            if np.any(too):
                ratio[too] = ratio[too] / (absr[too]*1.05)
            R[mask] = 1.0 + freq_blend * (ratio - 1.0)
        # Avoid near-zero R? maybe
        A_new = A * np.sqrt(R)
        A_new = hermitize_spectrum(A_new)  # may be costly but okay? N=32768, copy. Could skip for speed. Maybe only if verbose? Hmm.
        a_ideal = np.fft.ifft(A_new).real[:n]
        # try blends
        blend = min(0.9, blend0 * (0.5 + 0.5*progress))
        best_trial = current
        best_trial_score = cur_score
        # We'll evaluate blends in order if improves; to save, only eval bigger if previous not improved? Use list.
        for bl in (blend, 0.15, 0.05):
            if bl <= 0.0: continue
            trial = (1.0-bl)*current + bl*a_ideal
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            ss = float(trial.sum())
            if ss < 1e-15: continue
            trial = trial / ss
            ct = fast_conv_N(trial, N, L)
            mt = float(ct.max())
            sc = 2*n*mt
            if sc < best_trial_score:
                best_trial = trial; best_trial_score = sc
                if sc <= current_score? cannot break? If first improves, smaller blends likely less aggressive maybe not lower. Break? Could continue maybe small improve. To save, break if bl==blend and improved? But maybe 0.15 even better if blend too high. We'll not break, only 3.
        if best_trial_score < cur_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            current = best_trial
            bad = 0
            # if improved best, stall already 0 else? set stall maybe min
            if best_trial_score >= best_score:
                # no new best but progressed
                pass
        else:
            bad += 1
            if bad > 40:
                bad = 0
                blend0 = max(0.05, blend0*0.75)
                freq_blend = min(0.85, freq_blend*1.15)
            if stall > 500:
                strength = 0.01 + 0.005*min(5, stall//1000)
                current = best.copy()
                pert = rng.normal(0.0, strength, n) * current
                current = np.maximum(current + pert, 0.0)
                cs = current.sum()
                if cs < 1e-15: current=best.copy()
                else: current=current/cs
                stall=0
        if verbose and it % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"spectral {it} {time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s score={best_score:.6f}")
    return best, best_score
```
`hermitize_spectrum` each iter copies complex N and slice; may slow. Do we need? It ensures real inverse. Since A and R should yield near Hermitian; small imag okay. Maybe skip hermitize for speed, but if not Hermitian, ifft real part corresponds to symmetrized. It might improve or not. The copy overhead may be similar to FFT; could double. We can make optional and only hermitize if N <= 32768? Actually all. We can skip by default and maybe add parameter? The real part of ifft of non-Hermitian is equivalent to ifft of Hermitian part automatically, so okay. The imaginary part ignored. The real sequence's spectrum is Hermitian part, not A_new; square may not equal target. But hermitize would give same as taking real part? For any complex X, ifft(X).real = ifft(hermitian_part(X)). So hermitize before ifft equivalent to real part after. So no need. Skip for speed. 

`smooth_descent` detailed code. Need use `fast_conv_N`. 
```
def smooth_descent(a0, time_limit, step_rate=2.5, temp_start=0.6, temp_end=0.08, decay_iters=8000, restart_iters=2000, verbose=False, seed=31):
   a=norm_sum(a0); n=...; L...; N...
   x=a.copy(); x_prev=x.copy()
   best=x.copy(); c=fast_conv_N(x,N,L); best_m=float(c.max()); best_score=2*n*best_m
   t0=...; it=0; stall=0; restart=0
   rng=...
   while time...
      it+=1
      if time limit break
      if it == 1: beta=0.0
      else: beta=(it-1.0)/(it+1.5)
      y = x + beta*(x - x_prev)
      y = np.maximum(y, 1e-15)
      sy = y.sum()
      if sy < 1e-15: y=x.copy()
      else: y = y/sy
      A = np.fft.rfft(y,N)
      c = np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
      c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
      cmax = float(c.max())
      cur_score = 2*n*cmax
      if cur_score < best_score:
          best = y.copy(); best_score=cur_score; best_m=cmax; stall=0
      else:
          stall += 1
      # weights
      frac = min(1.0, float(it)/float(max(1, restart_iters*3)))
      tf = temp_start + (temp_end-temp_start)*frac
      T = tf * cmax
      if T <= 1e-15: T=1e-12
      scaled = (c - cmax)/T
      # clip lower -60 upper 0
      w = np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0))
      ws = float(w.sum())
      if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 1e-300:
          w = np.ones(L)/float(L)
      else:
          w = w/ws
      W = np.fft.rfft(w,N)
      corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W, N)[:n]
      grad = 2.0*corr
      grad = np.maximum(grad, 0.0)
      grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
      gmin = float(grad.min())
      grange = float(grad.max()) - gmin
      if grange <= 1e-14:
          if stall > 10:
              x = best.copy()
              x = np.maximum(x, 1e-12)
              x = x / x.sum()
              x_prev = x.copy()
          continue
      scale = step_rate / (1.0 + float(it)/float(decay_iters))
      if scale > 4.0: scale = 4.0
      eta = scale / grange
      # cap eta? max exponent = scale, okay. But if grad values huge, eta small; if eta underflows? ok.
      log_y = np.log(np.maximum(y, 1e-300))
      shift = eta * (grad - gmin)
      # To prevent overflow (shift positive, exp negative) okay. If shift large? max <=4, safe. But if grad-gmin maybe NaN handled.
      cand = np.exp(log_y - shift)
      cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
      cs = cand.sum()
      if cs < 1e-15:
          cand = y.copy()
      else:
          cand = cand/cs
      # Evaluate candidate
      B = np.fft.rfft(cand, N)
      cc = np.fft.irfft(B*B, N)[:L]
      cc = np.maximum(cc,0.0)
      cand_m = float(cc.max())
      cand_score = 2*n*cand_m
      # optional halve if not improve
      accept = False
      trial = cand
      trial_score = cand_score
      if cand_score >= cur_score:
          shift2 = 0.35 * eta * (grad - gmin)
          cand2 = np.exp(log_y - shift2)
          cand2 = np.maximum(cand2,1e-300)
          cs2 = cand2.sum()
          if cs2 > 1e-15: cand2 = cand2/cs2
          B2 = np.fft.rfft(cand2,N)
          cc2 = np.fft.irfft(B2*B2,N)[:L]
          cc2 = np.maximum(cc2,0.0)
          cand2_m = float(cc2.max())
          cand2_score = 2*n*cand2_m
          if cand2_score < trial_score:
              trial = cand2; trial_score = cand2_score
      if trial_score < cur_score:
          accept = True
      elif trial_score < best_score * 1.0005 and rng.random() < 0.05:
          accept = True
      if accept:
          x_prev = x
          x = trial
      else:
          x_prev = x
          # if many rejects, maybe reset acceleration by x_prev=x already
      if verbose and it%1000==0: print
      if stall > restart_iters:
          strength = 0.02 + 0.01*min(5, restart)
          x = best.copy()
          pert = rng.normal(0.0, strength, n) * x
          x = np.maximum(x + pert, 1e-12)
          sx=x.sum()
          if sx<1e-15: x=best.copy()
          else: x=x/sx
          x_prev = x.copy()
          stall=0; restart +=1
          # slow temp? no
   return best,best_score
```
Potential issue: If `cand_score` uses trial not accepted, and `cur_score` from y, but x score might be lower/higher. Accepting if trial < cur_score (Nesterov predicted) okay.

def capacity_smoothing(a0, time_limit, margin=1.05, eta=2.0, seed=0):
   a=norm_sum; n,N,L; Aa=fft(a,N)
   b=a.copy(); best=b.copy(); best_score=score of b
   M=max conv(a) * margin
   for it:
      B=fft(b,N); vals=ifft(Aa*B).real[:L]; vals=maximum
      maxv=vals.max()
      # weights over active: w=softmax((vals - M)/ (0.1M))? or raw
      active=np.maximum(vals/M - 0.7,0); w=active**2; normalize
      W=fft(w,N); d=ifft(conj(Aa)*W).real[:n] # derivative wrt b? vals=Σ a_i b_{k-i}; derivative wrt b_k = a_{k-k?} =? ∂vals_k/∂b_j = a_{k-j}. So d_j=Σ_k w_k a_{k-j}. This is correlation of a and w but with a? Earlier corr if x=a,y=w: ifft(conj(Aa)*W)_j =Σ a_m w_{m+j}. We need a_{k-j}=a_{m} with k=m+j => same. Yes d=corr(a,w).
      b_new = b * exp(-eta*d_mean_normalized); normalize
      blend 0.5
      evaluate self score of b_new; if best update
   return best

Update `fast_conv_N`:
```
def fast_conv_N(a,N,L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    # If input had tiny negative, conv may have negative; clip max
    if c.size:
       # small negative due roundoff; but if negative large from negative input, clipping hides? okay
       c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```

Let's think about `make_sparse_initial` mode 1 use `rng.integers(1, n)` requires n>1. If n=1, handle.
```
def make_sparse_initial(n,m,mode,seed):
    n=int(max(1,n)); m=int(max(1,min(m,n)))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if mode==1 and n>1:
       idx=[]; seen=set(); t=0; limit=m*50
       A=int(rng.integers(1,n)); B=int(rng.integers(0,n))
       while len(idx)<m and t<limit:
          r=(A*t*t + B*t) % n
          if r not in seen:
             seen.add(r); idx.append(r)
          t+=1
       if len(idx)<m:
          # random fill from unseen; to avoid O(n) maybe choice until
          for _ in range(m-len(idx)):
             r=int(rng.integers(0,n))
             if r not in seen:
                seen.add(r); idx.append(r)
                if len(idx)==m: break
          if len(idx)<m:
             idx=list(range(m))
       idx=np.array(idx[:m], dtype=int)
    else:
       idx=rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
    weights=np.exp(0.25*rng.normal(size=idx.size)*0.5) # =0.125 sd?
    # maybe scale weights with index? mode 2: linear weights
    if mode==2:
       w=np.linspace(0.5,1.5,idx.size)
    elif mode==3:
       w=np.exp(np.linspace(0,1,idx.size)*0.5)
    else: w=weights
    a[idx]=w
    return norm_sum(a)
```
Good.

Implement carefully:
```
def peak_shave(a0, time_limit, seed=77, verbose=False):
    a=norm_sum(a0); n=...; N=L...
    best=a.copy(); c=fast; best_m=c.max(); best_score=2*n*best_m
    t0=...; stall=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    it=0
    while time < limit:
       it+=1
       if time limit break
       c=fast_conv_N(a,N,L)
       M=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*M
       if sc<best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; best_m=M; stall=0
       else: stall+=1
       if M<=1e-300: break
       k_top=min(8, L)
       if k_top <=0: break
       if k_top >= L:
          top=np.arange(L)
       else:
          top=np.argpartition(c, -k_top)[-k_top:]
          top=top[np.argsort(-c[top])]  # descending
       cost=np.zeros(n)
       for ll in top[:8]:
          li=int(ll)
          lo=max(0, li-n+1); hi=min(n-1, li)
          if lo <= hi:
             js=np.arange(lo, hi+1)
             cost[js] += a[li-js]
       dest_score = cost + 5.0*a
       dest_order = np.argsort(dest_score)
       improved=False
       for ll in top[:8]:
          if improved: break
          li=int(ll)
          lo=max(0, li-n+1); hi=min(n-1, li)
          if lo>hi: continue
          # We can limit to indices contributing most to this peak
          idx=np.arange(lo, hi+1)
          contrib=a[idx]*a[li-idx]
          # top 2 sources
          src_order=np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]
          for so in src_order[:2]:
             src=int(idx[so])
             if a[src] <= 1e-12: continue
             # pick up to 3 destinations
             for dpos in dest_order[:50]:
                dst=int(dpos)
                if dst==src: continue
                # try fractions
                for frac in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2):
                   trial=a.copy()
                   amt=trial[src]*frac
                   if amt<=0: continue
                   trial[src]-=amt
                   trial[dst]+=amt
                   trial=np.maximum(trial,0.0)
                   ss=trial.sum()
                   if ss<1e-15: continue
                   trial=trial/ss
                   ct=fast_conv_N(trial,N,L)
                   mt=float(ct.max())
                   if mt < M - 1e-15:
                       a=trial; M=mt; sc=2*n*M
                       if sc<best_score:
                           best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; best_m=M
                       improved=True; stall=0
                       break
                if improved: break
             if improved: break
          if improved: break
       if not improved:
          # random perturbation small if stall grows
          if stall > 30:
             src=int(rng.integers(n))
             if a[src] > 1e-10:
                dst=int(rng.integers(n))
                while dst==src and n>1: dst=int(rng.integers(n))
                frac=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.08))
                trial=a.copy(); amt=trial[src]*frac; trial[src]-=amt; trial[dst]+=amt; trial=np.maximum(trial,0); trial=trial/trial.sum()
                ct=fast_conv_N(trial,N,L); mt=ct.max()
                if mt < M:
                   a=trial; M=mt; sc=2*n*M
                   if sc<best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; best_m=M
                   stall=0
          if stall > 200:
             strength=0.02+0.005*(stall//400)
             a=best.copy()
             a=np.maximum(a*(1+strength*rng.normal(0,1,n)),1e-12)
             a=a/a.sum(); M=fast_conv_N(a,N,L).max(); sc=2*n*M
             stall=0
       if verbose and it%1000==0: print
    return best,best_score
```
This uses many full copies and FFT; but okay for 60s. Might be slow due to 5 fractions * 50 dst * 8 peaks = 2000 evaluations per iteration impossible. We limit: for each peak, for source top2, try destinations top3? Actually dest_order[:50] too many. Break after improvement; but if no improvement, loops 8 peaks*2 sources*50 dst*4 frac = 3200 FFT per outer iteration, too slow. Need reduce.
Use only top 3 peaks, top 2 sources, top 5 destinations, 3 fractions =>90 FFT per iter. Still maybe okay but 100 iter=9000 FFT. N=8192, maybe 9000*0.5ms=4.5s? Actually Python overhead, maybe 30s. okay.
Reduce to top 4 peaks, 2 sources, 5 dst, 3 fractions =120 FFT. Good.
Also after one improvement, restart outer. Good.

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)**2) for nonnegative integer step heights.

Strategy:
- Use the previous parent sequence when available.
- Use FFT-based full-convolution evaluation internally (fast, scale-invariant).
- Spectral GS-style projections: rebuild the squared-polynomial spectrum toward
  a flattened coefficient target, then take a spectral square root and project
  back to nonnegative sequences.
- Exact subgradient/mirror descent using softmax weights over all convolution
  coefficients. The gradient is computed with FFT correlation.
- Sparse/chirp initializations and coordinate peak shaving to escape local minima.
- Multi-length interpolation and polishing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def norm_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def fast_conv_N(a, N, L):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_internal(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float('inf')
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return float('inf')
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)
    c = fast_conv_N(a, N, L)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float('inf')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float('inf')
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def safe_evaluate(seq):
    try:
        out = [float(x) for x in seq]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    f = norm_sum(f)
    if f.size == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    if f.size == 1:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, f.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        out = np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    else:
        out = out / s
    return out


def initial_shape(n, kind=0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if kind == 0:
        return norm_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    if kind == 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        return norm_sum(1.0 + 0.5 * x)
    if kind == 2:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        return norm_sum(1.0 + x)
    if kind == 3:
        i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        return norm_sum(i ** 0.35)
    if kind == 4:
        i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        return norm_sum(i ** 0.7)
    if kind == 5:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        return norm_sum(np.exp(-((x - 0.4) / 0.25) ** 2))
    if kind == 6:
        L = 2 * n - 1
        N = next_pow2(L)
        target = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        C = np.fft.fft(target, N)
        A = np.sqrt(C + 0j)
        a = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
        return norm_sum(a)
    return norm_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))


def make_sparse_initial(n, m, mode=0, seed=1):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = int(max(1, min(m, n)))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if mode == 1 and n > 1:
        idx = []
        seen = set()
        t = 0
        limit = m * 50
        Acoef = int(rng.integers(1, n))
        Bcoef = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        while len(idx) < m and t < limit:
            r = (Acoef * t * t + Bcoef * t) % n
            if r not in seen:
                seen.add(r)
                idx.append(int(r))
            t += 1
        if len(idx) < m:
            for _ in range(m - len(idx)):
                r = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if r not in seen:
                    seen.add(r)
                    idx.append(r)
                    if len(idx) == m:
                        break
        if len(idx) < m:
            idx = list(range(m))
        idx = np.array(idx[:m], dtype=int)
    else:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
        idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=int)

    if mode == 2:
        w = np.linspace(0.5, 1.5, idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
    elif mode == 3:
        w = np.exp(0.5 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, idx.size, dtype=np.float64))
    else:
        w = np.exp(0.2 * rng.normal(size=idx.size))
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 1.0)
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)

    a[idx] = w
    return norm_sum(a)


def spectral_gs(a0, time_limit, alpha0=0.30, alpha1=0.85,
                freq_blend=0.35, blend0=0.35, verbose=False, seed=17):
    a = norm_sum(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float('inf')
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)

    best = a.copy()
    c0 = fast_conv_N(a, N, L)
    best_m = float(np.max(c0))
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_m)

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    current = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    bad = 0
    anneal = 800
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break
        it += 1

        A = np.fft.fft(current, N)
        S = A * A
        c = np.fft.ifft(S).real[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        cur_m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * cur_m)

        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score:
            best = current.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            best_m = cur_m
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if cur_m <= 1e-300:
            break

        progress = min(1.0, float(it) / float(max(1, anneal)))
        alpha = alpha0 + (alpha1 - alpha0) * progress

        mean_c = float(np.mean(c)) if c.size else 1.0 / float(L)
        if not np.isfinite(mean_c) or mean_c <= 0.0:
            mean_c = 1.0 / float(L)
        eps = max(1e-12, 1e-8 * mean_c)

        target = np.power(c + eps, alpha)
        target = target + (0.02 / float(L))
        ts = float(np.sum(target))
        if not np.isfinite(ts) or ts <= 0.0:
            target = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            target = target / ts

        Ct = np.fft.fft(target, N)
        R = np.ones(N, dtype=np.complex128)
        mask = np.abs(S) > 1e-12
        if np.any(mask):
            ratio = Ct[mask] / S[mask]
            absr = np.abs(ratio)
            too_big = absr > 50.0
            if np.any(too_big):
                ratio = ratio.copy()
                ratio[too_big] = ratio[too_big] / (absr[too_big] * 1.05)
            R[mask] = 1.0 + freq_blend * (ratio - 1.0)

        R = np.where(np.abs(R) < 1e-8, 1.0 + 0j, R)
        R = np.nan_to_num(R, nan=1.0 + 0j, posinf=50.0 + 0j, neginf=50.0 + 0j)

        A_new = A * np.sqrt(R)
        a_ideal = np.fft.ifft(A_new).real[:n]
        a_ideal = np.where(np.isfinite(a_ideal), a_ideal, 0.0)

        blend = min(0.9, blend0 * (0.5 + 0.5 * progress))
        best_trial = current
        best_trial_score = cur_score

        for bl in (blend, 0.15, 0.05):
            if bl <= 0.0:
                continue
            trial = (1.0 - bl) * current + bl * a_ideal
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            ss = float(np.sum(trial))
            if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss < 1e-15:
                continue
            trial = trial / ss
            ct = fast_conv_N(trial, N, L)
            mt = float(np.max(ct)) if ct.size else 0.0
            sc = float(2.0 * n * mt)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score:
                best_trial = trial
                best_trial_score = sc

        if np.isfinite(best_trial_score) and best_trial_score < cur_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            current = best_trial
            bad = 0
        else:
            bad += 1
            if bad > 40:
                bad = 0
                blend0 = max(0.05, blend0 * 0.75)
                freq_blend = min(0.85, freq_blend * 1.15)
            if stall > 500:
                strength = 0.01 + 0.005 * min(5, int(stall // 1000))
                current = best.copy()
                current = np.maximum(current + rng.normal(0.0, strength, n), 1e-12)
                cs = float(current.sum())
                if cs < 1e-15:
                    current = best.copy()
                else:
                    current = current / cs
                stall = 0

        if verbose and it % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"spectral_gs it={it} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s best={best_score:.6f}")

    return best, best_score


def smooth_descent(a0, time_limit, step_rate=2.5, temp_start=0.6, temp_end=0.08,
                   decay_iters=8000, restart_iters=2000, verbose=False, seed=31):
    a = norm_sum(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float('inf')
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)

    x = a.copy()
    x_prev = a.copy()
    best = a.copy()
    c0 = fast_conv_N(a, N, L)
    best_m = float(np.max(c0))
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_m)

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    restart = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break
        it += 1

        if it == 1:
            beta = 0.0
        else:
            beta = (it - 1.0) / (it + 1.5)

        y = x + beta * (x - x_prev)
        y = np.maximum(y, 1e-15)
        sy = float(np.sum(y))
        if not np.isfinite(sy) or sy < 1e-15:
            y = x.copy()
        else:
            y = y / sy

        A = np.fft.rfft(y, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)

        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score:
            best = y.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            best_m = cmax
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if cmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        frac = min(1.0, float(it) / float(max(1, restart_iters * 3)))
        tf = temp_start + (temp_end - temp_start) * frac
        T = tf * cmax
        if not np.isfinite(T) or T <= 1e-15:
            T = 1e-12

        scaled = (c - cmax) / T
        w = np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0))
        ws = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws < 1e-300:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            w = w / ws

        W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
        corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * W, N)[:n]
        grad = 2.0 * corr
        grad = np.maximum(grad, 0.0)
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)

        gmin = float(np.min(grad))
        grange = float(np.max(grad)) - gmin
        if not np.isfinite(grange) or grange <= 1e-14:
            if stall > 10:
                x = np.maximum(best, 1e-12)
                sx = float(x.sum())
                if sx < 1e-15:
                    x = best.copy()
                else:
                    x = x / sx
                x_prev = x.copy()
            continue

        scale = step_rate / (1.0 + float(it) / float(max(1, decay_iters)))
        if scale > 4.0:
            scale = 4.0
        eta = scale / grange

        log_y = np.log(np.maximum(y, 1e-300))
        shift = eta * (grad - gmin)
        cand = np.exp(log_y - shift)
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
        cs = float(np.sum(cand))
        if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs < 1e-15:
            cand = y.copy()
        else:
            cand = cand / cs

        B = np.fft.rfft(cand, N)
        cc = np.fft.irfft(B * B, N)[:L]
        cc = np.maximum(cc, 0.0)
        cand_m = float(np.max(cc)) if cc.size else 0.0
        cand_score = float(2.0 * n * cand_m)

        trial = cand
        trial_score = cand_score

        if cand_score >= cur_score:
            shift2 = 0.35 * eta * (grad - gmin)
            cand2 = np.exp(log_y - shift2)
            cand2 = np.maximum(cand2, 1e-300)
            cs2 = float(np.sum(cand2))
            if np.isfinite(cs2) and cs2 > 1e-15:
                cand2 = cand2 / cs2
                B2 = np.fft.rfft(cand2, N)
                cc2 = np.fft.irfft(B2 * B2, N)[:L]
                cc2 = np.maximum(cc2, 0.0)
                cand2_m = float(np.max(cc2)) if cc2.size else 0.0
                cand2_score = float(2.0 * n * cand2_m)
                if np.isfinite(cand2_score) and cand2_score < trial_score:
                    trial = cand2
                    trial_score = cand2_score

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(trial_score) and trial_score < cur_score:
            accept = True
        elif np.isfinite(trial_score) and trial_score < best_score * 1.0005 and rng.random() < 0.05:
            accept = True

        if accept:
            x_prev = x
            x = trial
        else:
            x_prev = x

        if verbose and it % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"smooth_descent it={it} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s best={best_score:.6f}")

        if stall > restart_iters:
            strength = 0.02 + 0.01 * min(5, restart)
            x = best.copy()
            x = np.maximum(x + rng.normal(0.0, strength, n), 1e-12)
            sx = float(x.sum())
            if sx < 1e-15:
                x = best.copy()
            else:
                x = x / sx
            x_prev = x.copy()
            stall = 0
            restart += 1

    return best, best_score


def peak_shave(a0, time_limit, seed=77, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float('inf')
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)

    best = a.copy()
    c0 = fast_conv_N(a, N, L)
    best_m = float(np.max(c0))
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_m)

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break
        it += 1

        c = fast_conv_N(a, N, L)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            best_m = M
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if M <= 1e-300:
            break

        k_top = min(6, L)
        if k_top <= 0:
            break

        if k_top >= L:
            top = np.arange(L)
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -k_top)[-k_top:]
            top = top[np.argsort(-c[top])]

        cost = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for ll in top[:6]:
            li = int(ll)
            lo = max(0, li - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, li)
            if lo <= hi:
                js = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
                cost[js] += a[li - js]

        dest_score = cost + 5.0 * a
        dest_order = np.argsort(dest_score)

        improved = False
        for ll in top[:4]:
            if improved:
                break
            li = int(ll)
            lo = max(0, li - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, li)
            if lo > hi:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
            contrib = a[idx] * a[li - idx]
            src_order = np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]

            for so in src_order[:2]:
                if improved:
                    break
                src = int(idx[so])
                if a[src] <= 1e-12:
                    continue

                for dpos in dest_order[:6]:
                    if improved:
                        break
                    dst = int(dpos)
                    if dst == src:
                        continue

                    for frac in (0.02, 0.05, 0.12):
                        trial = a.copy()
                        amt = trial[src] * frac
                        if amt <= 0.0:
                            continue
                        trial[src] -= amt
                        trial[dst] += amt
                        trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                        ss = float(trial.sum())
                        if ss < 1e-15:
                            continue
                        trial = trial / ss
                        ct = fast_conv_N(trial, N, L)
                        mt = float(np.max(ct)) if ct.size else 0.0
                        if mt < M - 1e-15:
                            a = trial
                            M = mt
                            sc = float(2.0 * n * M)
                            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                                best = a.copy()
                                best_score = sc
                                best_m = M
                            improved = True
                            stall = 0
                            break
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if not improved:
            if stall > 30 and n > 1:
                src = int(rng.integers(n))
                dst = int(rng.integers(n))
                while dst == src:
                    dst = int(rng.integers(n))
                if a[src] > 1e-10:
                    frac = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.08))
                    trial = a.copy()
                    amt = trial[src] * frac
                    trial[src] -= amt
                    trial[dst] += amt
                    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                    ss = float(trial.sum())
                    if ss > 1e-15:
                        trial = trial / ss
                        ct = fast_conv_N(trial, N, L)
                        mt = float(np.max(ct)) if ct.size else 0.0
                        if mt < M:
                            a = trial
                            M = mt
                            sc = float(2.0 * n * M)
                            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                                best = a.copy()
                                best_score = sc
                                best_m = M
                            stall = 0

            if stall > 200:
                strength = 0.02 + 0.005 * min(5, int(stall // 400))
                a = best.copy()
                a = np.maximum(a + rng.normal(0.0, strength, n), 1e-12)
                assum = float(a.sum())
                if assum < 1e-15:
                    a = best.copy()
                else:
                    a = a / assum
                M = float(np.max(fast_conv_N(a, N, L)))
                stall = 0

        if verbose and it % 500 == 0:
            print(f"peak_shave it={it} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s best={best_score:.6f}")

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(max(120.0, budget_s))
    hard = min(total_budget - 45.0, 1755.0)
    if hard < 120.0:
        hard = max(60.0, total_budget * 0.9)

    best = None
    best_score = float('inf')
    MAXN = 12000

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
        if parent.size > 0 and float(parent.sum()) > 1e-15:
            p = norm_sum(parent)
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = score_internal(p)
            print(f"Loaded parent: n={p.size}, internal_score={best_score:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        print("No parent sequence found; using bootstrap.")

    # Bootstrap if no useful parent.
    if best is None:
        rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
        n_boot = 6000
        kinds = [6, 3, 4, 0, 1, 5, 2]
        for kind in kinds:
            rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            if rem < 20:
                break
            cand = initial_shape(n_boot, kind)
            sc = score_internal(cand)
            if rem > 15:
                tt = min(6.0, rem * 0.25)
                out, s = spectral_gs(cand, tt, alpha0=0.2, alpha1=0.8,
                                     freq_blend=0.4, blend0=0.4, seed=7 + kind)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < sc:
                    cand = out
                    sc = s
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = cand
                best_score = sc
        if best is None:
            best = initial_shape(n_boot, 0)
            best_score = score_internal(best)
        rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
        if rem > 60:
            out, s = smooth_descent(best, min(50.0, rem * 0.5),
                                    step_rate=3.0, temp_start=0.4, temp_end=0.05,
                                    seed=11)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best = out
                best_score = s

    if best is None:
        best = initial_shape(5000, 0)
        best_score = score_internal(best)

    if best.size > MAXN:
        best = interpolate_sequence(best, MAXN)
        best_score = score_internal(best)
        print(f"Downsampled to n={best.size}, internal_score={best_score:.6f}")

    # ---- Multi-resolution shape transfer ----
    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    n_main = best.size
    if rem > 120 and n_main > 256:
        print(f"Phase small-resolution: n_small from {n_main}")
        n_small = max(128, min(800, n_main // 4))
        work = interpolate_sequence(best, n_small)

        tt = min(70.0, rem * 0.3)
        out, s = spectral_gs(work, tt, alpha0=0.2, alpha1=0.8,
                             freq_blend=0.4, blend0=0.45, seed=123)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            work_small = out
            back = interpolate_sequence(out, n_main)
            sc = score_internal(back)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = back.copy()
                best_score = sc
        else:
            work_small = work

        rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
        if rem > 70:
            tt = min(50.0, rem * 0.5)
            out, s = smooth_descent(work_small, tt, step_rate=2.5,
                                    temp_start=0.4, temp_end=0.06, seed=124)
            back = interpolate_sequence(out, n_main)
            sc = score_internal(back)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = back.copy()
                best_score = sc

    # ---- Main spectral and smooth-max polishing ----
    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 80:
        print(f"\nPhase spectral main: n={best.size}")
        tt = min(250.0, rem * 0.35)
        out, s = spectral_gs(best, tt, alpha0=0.25, alpha1=0.85,
                             freq_blend=0.30, blend0=0.35, seed=202)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = out
            best_score = s
            print(f"  spectral main improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 80:
        print(f"\nPhase smooth main: n={best.size}")
        tt = min(300.0, rem * 0.4)
        out, s = smooth_descent(best, tt, step_rate=2.5,
                                temp_start=0.6, temp_end=0.08,
                                decay_iters=8000, restart_iters=2000, seed=303)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = out
            best_score = s
            print(f"  smooth main improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 60:
        print(f"\nPhase spectral mild: n={best.size}")
        tt = min(150.0, rem * 0.35)
        out, s = spectral_gs(best, tt, alpha0=0.45, alpha1=0.95,
                             freq_blend=0.18, blend0=0.25, seed=404)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = out
            best_score = s
            print(f"  spectral mild improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 80:
        print(f"\nPhase smooth aggressive: n={best.size}")
        tt = min(200.0, rem * 0.35)
        out, s = smooth_descent(best, tt, step_rate=3.5,
                                temp_start=0.25, temp_end=0.03,
                                decay_iters=5000, restart_iters=1500, seed=505)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = out
            best_score = s
            print(f"  smooth aggressive improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    # ---- Sparse / chirp trials ----
    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 100:
        print(f"\nPhase sparse trials")
        n_sparse = best.size
        if n_sparse > 8000:
            n_sparse = 5000
        n_sparse = max(2000, min(MAXN, n_sparse))
        base = float(np.sqrt(float(n_sparse)))
        m_vals = [int(1.2 * base), int(2.0 * base), int(3.5 * base)]

        mi = 0
        for mval in m_vals:
            rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            if rem < 60:
                break
            for mode in (1, 0):
                rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
                if rem < 30:
                    break
                seed = 1000 + mi * 10 + mode
                cand = make_sparse_initial(n_sparse, mval, mode, seed=seed)
                tt = min(14.0, rem * 0.25)
                out, s = smooth_descent(cand, tt, step_rate=3.5,
                                        temp_start=0.3, temp_end=0.05,
                                        decay_iters=3000, restart_iters=1000,
                                        seed=seed + 100)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                    best = out
                    best_score = s
                    print(f"  sparse n={n_sparse}, m={mval}, mode={mode} improved: {best_score:.6f}")
                rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            mi += 1

    # ---- Longer-length interpolation / refinement ----
    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 120:
        print(f"\nPhase longer lengths")
        tried = set()
        tried.add(int(best.size))
        n_cur = int(best.size)
        cands = []
        for mult in (1.25, 1.6, 2.0):
            nn = int(n_cur * mult)
            if nn > MAXN:
                nn = MAXN
            if nn > 1500 and nn not in tried:
                tried.add(nn)
                cands.append(nn)
        for add in (6000, 8000, 10000, 12000):
            if add > 1500 and add not in tried:
                tried.add(add)
                cands.append(add)
        cands = sorted(set(cands))

        for idx, nn in enumerate(cands[:3]):
            rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            if rem < 120:
                break
            print(f"  Trying n={nn}")
            work = interpolate_sequence(best, nn)
            work_score = score_internal(work)
            if np.isfinite(work_score) and work_score < best_score:
                best = work.copy()
                best_score = work_score

            tt = min(130.0, rem * 0.4)
            out, s = spectral_gs(work, tt, alpha0=0.30, alpha1=0.90,
                                 freq_blend=0.30, blend0=0.40, seed=3000 + nn)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                if s < work_score:
                    work = out
                    work_score = s
                if work_score < best_score:
                    best = work.copy()
                    best_score = work_score
                    print(f"    spectral n={nn} improved: {best_score:.6f}")

            rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            if rem > 60:
                tt = min(130.0, rem * 0.4)
                out, s = smooth_descent(work, tt, step_rate=3.0,
                                        temp_start=0.4, temp_end=0.05,
                                        decay_iters=6000, restart_iters=1800,
                                        seed=4000 + nn)
                if np.isfinite(s):
                    if s < work_score:
                        work = out
                        work_score = s
                    if work_score < best_score:
                        best = work.copy()
                        best_score = work_score
                        print(f"    smooth n={nn} improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    # ---- Final coordinate peak shaving and intensive polish ----
    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 80:
        print(f"\nPhase peak_shave: n={best.size}")
        tt = min(90.0, rem * 0.5)
        out, s = peak_shave(best, tt, seed=888)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = out
            best_score = s
            print(f"  peak_shave improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 50:
        print(f"\nPhase final spectral very mild: n={best.size}")
        tt = min(80.0, rem * 0.5)
        out, s = spectral_gs(best, tt, alpha0=0.55, alpha1=0.97,
                             freq_blend=0.12, blend0=0.18, seed=909)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = out
            best_score = s
            print(f"  final spectral improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 50:
        print(f"\nPhase final smooth sharp: n={best.size}")
        tt = min(150.0, rem * 0.7)
        out, s = smooth_descent(best, tt, step_rate=4.0,
                                temp_start=0.18, temp_end=0.02,
                                decay_iters=4000, restart_iters=1200, seed=1010)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = out
            best_score = s
            print(f"  final smooth improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    rem = hard - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 30:
        tt = min(60.0, rem)
        out, s = peak_shave(best, tt, seed=1111)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = out
            best_score = s
            print(f"  final peak_shave improved: {best_score:.6f}")

    # Sanitize and optionally validate with the official evaluator.
    best = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best = np.where(np.isfinite(best), best, 0.0)
    best = np.maximum(best, 0.0)
    best = np.minimum(best, 1000.0)
    bs = float(best.sum())
    if best.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(bs) or bs < 0.01:
        best = initial_shape(4200, 0)
        bs = float(best.sum())
    else:
        best = best / bs

    official = safe_evaluate(best)
    internal = score_internal(best)
    if not np.isfinite(official):
        print("Official evaluation invalid; falling back to uniform-safe sequence.")
        best = initial_shape(4200, 0)
        official = safe_evaluate(best)
        internal = score_internal(best)

    print(f"\nFINAL n={best.size} internal={internal:.6f} official={official:.6f} "
          f"time={time.monotonic()-t_global:.1f}s")

    return [float(x) for x in best]
```
